    Company Profile
    公司簡介

    WEILONG Delicious Global Holdings Ltd (the “Company”) and its                衛龍美味全球控股有限公司（「本公司」）及其附屬公司
    subsidiaries (“the Group”, “we” or “us”) are a leading spicy snack food      （「本集團」或「我們」）是中國領先的集研發、生產及銷
    enterprise integrating R&D, production and sales in China. The Group was     售為一體的辣味休閒食品企業。本集團於2022年12月
    listed on the Main Board of the Hong Kong Stock Exchange (stock code:        15日在香港聯交所主板上市（股份代號：9985.HK）。
    9985.HK) on December 15, 2022.


    Based on traditional formulas, the Group started the Chinese seasoned        本集團以傳統美食為基礎開創了中國調味麵製品（俗
    flour products (also commonly known as Latiao (辣條)) industry, created        稱「辣條」）行業，於2001年開創出第一根辣條，並引
    our first Latiao (辣條) snack in 2001, leading the standards construction of   領了辣條行業標準建設。憑藉出眾的產品拓展能力，
    Latiao industry. With our outstanding product development capabilities,      本集團已成功擴展至蔬菜製品及其它產品等品類，推
    the Group has successfully diversified into vegetable products and others,   出了包括魔芋爽、風吃海帶等大單品。本年度，本集
    including Konjac Shuang (魔芋爽), Fengchi Kelp (風吃海帶) and others.               團又推出麻醬口味、高纖牛肝菌口味及傣味舂雞腳口
    The Group has further launched a variety of new products like sesame         味等多款「魔芋爽」、麻辣牛肉口味「親嘴燒」等多款新
    paste-flavoured, high-fiber Porcini-flavoured, and spicy Dai (Yunnan) –      品，深受消費者的喜愛。
    style pounded chicken-feet flavoured variants Konjac Shuang (魔芋爽),
    spicy beef-flavoured Kiss Burn (親嘴燒) in the Current Year, which are
    quite popular among consumers.


    Adhering to our product development philosophy of “maximizing the            秉承「以工業化思維，讓美食原點的價值最大化釋放」
    intrinsic value of nature with an industrialized approach”, the Group        的產品開發理念，本集團持續升級現有產品及推出新
    continuously upgrades existing products and launches new products,           產品，專注品質，建立了行業領先的生產體系及嚴格
    focuses on quality through establishing an industry-leading production       的品質保障體系。
    system and strict quality assurance system.


    We are a popular snack food brand among young consumers in China.            我們是倍受中國年輕消費者喜愛的休閑食品品牌，本
    The Group focuses and understands the needs of young consumer                集團深度洞察年輕消費群體的需求，不斷運用新穎有
    groups, constantly enhances brand value through novel and interesting        趣的互動玩法提升品牌價值，並緊跟時下熱點話題趨
    interactions with consumers, and keeps up with current hot topic trends      勢，賦予品牌新活力。本年度，我們榮登中商產業研
    to give the brand new vitality. In 2025, we were honored to be named         究院「2025年中國最受消費者喜愛的十大辣味休閑食
    No.1 on the list of “2025 Top Ten Spicy Leisure Food Brands Loved by         品品牌」榜首，成功入選「胡潤中國食品行業百強榜」
    Chinese Consumers” by ASKCI Industrial Research Institute, selected for      及證券之星2025「未來力量」新消費品牌榜等多項獎
    the “Hurun China Food Industry Top 100 List,” and included in Stock          項。
    Star’s 2025 “Future Power” New Consumer Brand List, among others.


    The Group has an omni-channel sales and distribution network that            本集團擁有有效觸達年輕消費者的全渠道銷售及經銷
    effectively reaches consumers, we have quickly adapted to the evolving       網絡，緊隨消費者購買行為的演變，我們迅速擴張到
    purchasing behavior of consumers and expanded to supermarkets,               廣泛覆蓋消費者的商超、連鎖便利店、傳統電商及新
    chained convenience stores, traditional e-commerce channels and              興的零食量販店、內容電商、社交電商等渠道。
    emerging snacks specialty retailers, content e-commerce channels
    and social e-commerce channels which have wide exposure to young
    consumers.

affordable, digitally intelligent, to achieve leadership in NPS and         1
                                                                                態平台」的美好願景，堅持「以消費者體驗為中心，以

joy and happiness to people for 123 years”. We adhere to the core value         觀，持續不斷地為消費者提供更好的產品和愉悅的消
of “putting consumer experience at the core, valuing those who strive,          費體驗。
benchmarking courageously against best-in-class peers, and embracing
continuous self-reflection”, continuously offer consumers better products
and a cheerful consumption experience.

Definitions 釋義




“ERP system”                    enterprise resource planning systems, which is a business process management
                                system consisting of integrated software applications that help manage a business
                                and automate many back office functions related to technology, service and human
                                resources
「ERP系統」                         企業資源規劃系統，一種包含一體化軟件應用程序的業務流程管理系統，可協助管
                                理業務並將多項技術、服務及人力資源相關的後台功能自動化

“ESG”                           environmental, social and governance
「ESG」                           環境、社會及管治

”FSSC 22000”                    Food Safety System Certification, a food safety management certification scheme
                                that consists of three components: ISO 22000, sector specific Pre-Requisite Programs
                                and additional requirements. Besides these three components there is a FSSC
                                22000-Quality option based on the additional requirements of ISO 9001
「FSSC 22000」                    食 品 安 全 體 系 認 證 ， 一 項 包 含 三 個 組 成 部 分 的 食 品 安 全 管 理 認 證 計 劃 ， 即 ISO
                                22000、特定行業的前提方案和附加要求。除該等三個組成部分外，另有基於ISO
                                9001附加要求的FSSC 22000－質量選項

“Group” or “our Group” or       our Company and its subsidiaries (or our Company and any one or more of its
  “we” or “us”                  subsidiaries, as the context may require)
「本集團」或「我們」                      本公司及其附屬公司（或如文義所指，本公司及其任何一家或多家附屬公司）

“HACCP”                         a quality management system which identifies and evaluates points during production
                                in order to set up measures and control hazards to ensure product safety
「HACCP」                         一種質量管理體系，通過識別及評估生產過程中的臨界點，制定多項措施並控制危
                                害，從而確保產品安全

“HH Global Capital”             HH Global Capital Ltd
「和和全球資本」                        和和全球資本有限公司

     Management Discussion and Analysis
     管理層討論與分析

     MACRO AND INDUSTRY ENVIRONMENT                                                   宏觀及行業環境
     Looking back at 2025, amid a complex and evolving domestic and                   回顧2025年，面對錯綜複雜的國內外環境，中國經濟
     international environment, China’s economy maintained overall stability          運行總體平穩，實現全年5.0%的增長目標，充分展
     and achieved its full-year growth target of 5.0%, fully demonstrating            現了國內經濟在內外部挑戰下的韌性和潛能。經濟的
     the resilience and potential of the domestic economy amid internal and           穩健增長、消費的持續回暖與內需拉動作用的顯著增
     external challenges. Robust economic growth, the continued recovery              強，為中國經濟高質量發展注入了持久動能，也為消
     in consumption, and the notably strengthened driving role of domestic            費市場信心的穩固築牢了堅實基礎。
     demand have injected sustained momentum into China’s high-quality
     economic development and laid a solid foundation for bolstering
     consumer market confidence.

     China’s consumer market is of enormous scale. In 2025, the total retail          中國消費市場規模龐大，2025年全國社會消費品零
     sales of consumer goods nationwide exceeded RMB50 trillion for the               售總額首次突破人民幣50萬億元，消費對經濟增長的
     first time, with consumption contributing 52% to economic growth.                貢獻達52%，在投資與出口承壓的背景下，消費已成
     Amid mounting pressure on investment and exports, consumption                    為經濟增長的主動力，為食品飲料行業提供了堅實的
     has emerged as the primary engine of economic growth, laying a solid             市場基礎1。麥肯錫在《2025消費者狀況：當顛覆成為
     market foundation for the food and beverage industry1. As highlighted            常態》2報告中指出，全球消費者的消費行為已與五年
     in McKinsey’s report, State of the Consumer 2025: When Disruption                前顯著不同，更注重即時滿足、便利性與自我關注。
     Becomes the Permanent 2 , global consumer behavior has shifted                   在此趨勢下，把握一代代年輕消費群體的需求成為品
     significantly compared to five years ago, with greater emphasis placed           牌實現長期增長的重要機遇。此外，消費者對國民品
     on instant gratification, convenience, and self-care. In light of this trend,    牌的青睞度持續提升，為國民品牌帶來新的結構性機
     capturing the demands of successive generations of young consumers               遇，也驅動品牌持續創新，以更快速、更精準地回應
     presents a critical opportunity for brands to achieve long-term growth.          市場與消費者的變化。
     Furthermore, consumers’ growing preference for domestic brands has
     created new structural opportunities for homegrown brands, driving them
     to continuously innovate and respond to market and consumer shifts with
     greater speed and precision.

     Under the dual drivers of consumption upgrading and channel innovation,          在消費升級與渠道革新的雙重驅動下，中國休閒食品
     China’s snack food industry has steadily expanded in scale, with                 行業規模穩步擴容，消費場景持續拓寬。根據iiMedia
     continuously broadening consumption scenarios. According to the latest           Research（ 艾媒諮詢）最新發佈的《2025年中國休閒食
     “2025 China Snack Food Industry Development Status and Consumer                  品行業發展狀況與消費行為調查數據》3顯示，2025年
     Behavior Survey Data” released by iiMedia Research (a leading third-             中國休閒食品行業規模約為人民幣11,804億元，2027
     party data mining and analysis institution for the new economy industry)3,       年預計將達到人民幣12,378億元。普華永道在《2025
     the scale of China’s snack food industry is expected to reach 1,180.4            全球消費者之聲中國報告－新時代中國消費者：聚焦

     OUR FOOD SAFETY AND QUALITY CONTROL                                            我們的食品安全和品質控制
     As one of China’s leading snack food enterprises, we always take quality       作為中國領先的休閒食品企業，我們始終以質量為
     as our core competitiveness and regard food safety as the “lifeline” of        核心競爭力，將食品安全視為企業生存發展的「生命
     our survival and development. We continuously refine and upgrade our           線」，持續完善並升級食品安全管理體系，從原料甄
     food safety management system, enforcing strict controls across the            選到生產交付全流程嚴格管控，確保為消費者提供安

     ensuring that consumers are provided with safe, reliable, and high-quality     需求升級趨勢，積極推進創新研發，聚焦美味化、健
     snack foods. Meanwhile, building on the upgrading trend of consumer            康化、多元化產品迭代，以高品質供給贏得市場廣泛
     demand, we vigorously promote innovation and R&D, focusing on tastier,         信任與長期支持。
     healthier and more diversified product iterations. By providing high-quality
     offerings, we have earned widespread market trust and long-term
     support.

GOING CONCERN                                                                     持續經營
On the basis of current financial projections and financing available,            根據現行財務預測和可動用的融資，本集團在可見未
the Group has adequate financial resources to continue its operation in           來有足夠財務資源繼續經營。因此在編製財務報告時
the foreseeable future. Accordingly, the financial statements have been           已採用持續經營基準編製。
prepared on a going concern basis.

all the Directors and employees (including but not limited to permanent,        限於永久、全職、兼職和合同制員工等）以及與集團

parties who deal with the Group (including but not limited to investors,        戶、承包商、供應商、債權人和債務人等）的檢舉政
customers, contractors, suppliers, creditors and debtors, etc.). The policy     策。該政策旨在為員工和任何外部方提供保密的舉報
is designed to provide the employees and any external parties with              渠道以向集團報告公司財務報告、內部控制或其他領
confidential whistleblowing channels to report to the Group the actual          域的實際或疑似非法活動和不當行為。截至2025年12
or suspected illegal activities and misconducts in corporate financial          月31日止年度，本公司概無發現任何非法活動及不當

ANTI-CORRUPTION POLICY                                                          反貪政策
The Group does not tolerate any corruption, bribery, extortion, fraud or        本集團在其業務活動中絕不容忍任何貪污、賄賂、勒
money laundering during the course of its business activities. As such,         索、欺詐或洗錢行為。為此，本集團參考有關法例，
it has formulated an anti-corruption policy which prohibits all forms           制定了一項反貪政策，禁止一切形式的貪污行為。
of corruption practice by making reference to the relevant laws and             《反貪政策》是本集團企業管治架構的重要組成部分，
regulations. The anti-corruption policy forms an integral part of the           訂明本集團員工在打擊貪污時必須遵守的具體行為指
Group’s corporate governance framework, which sets out the specific             引。我們定期檢討和更新《反貪政策》，以配合適用的
behavioural guidelines that the employees of the Group must follow to           法律法規和業界的最佳慣例。
combat corruption. The anti-corruption policy is reviewed and updated on
a regular basis to align with the applicable laws and regulations as well as
the industry best practices.

     RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROL                                         風險管理及內部控制
     The Board is aware of its responsibility for risk management and internal    董事會知悉其有關風險管理及內部控制系統，並至少
     control systems and reviews their effectiveness at least once annually.      每年審閱一次其有效性的責任。該系統旨在管理而非
     The system is designed to manage, rather than eliminate, the risk of         消除無法實現業務目標的風險，促進有效及高效運
     failure to achieve business objectives, to promote effective and efficient   營，合理保證財務報告可靠性及遵守適用法律及法規
     operations, to reasonably guarantee the reliability of financial reporting   以及保障本公司資產，且僅可就重大錯報或損失提供
     and compliance with applicable laws and regulations, and to safeguard        合理而非絕對的保證。
     the assets of the Company, and the system only to provide a reasonable,
     but not absolute, guarantee against material misstatement or loss.


     The Board has overall responsibility for assessing and determining           董事會全面負責評估及釐定其在實現本公司策略目標
     the nature and extent of the risks it is willing to take in achieving        及建立及維護適宜且有效的風險管理及內部控制系統
     the Company’s strategic objectives and establishing and maintaining          方面願意承擔的風險的性質和程度。
     appropriate and effective risk management and internal control systems.


     Characteristics of the Risk Management and Internal                          風險管理和內部監控組織體系特點
     Control Organization System
     In accordance with the requirements of Code Provision D.2.2 under            根據《企業管治守則》第二部分第D.2.2條的要求，為
     Section II of the Corporate Governance Code, the Company has established     確保本公司在風險管理和內部審核方面具備足夠的資
     a sound risk management and internal control organization system which       源、員工資歷及經驗、足夠的培訓課程和有關預算，
     includes the Board, the Audit Committee, the office of the Board, the        本公司建立了完善的風險管理和內部監控組織體系，
     audit center, the internal control department, the legal department and      包括董事會、審核委員會、董事會辦公室、審計中
     other departments to ensure that the Company has sufficient resources,       心、內控部、法務部以及其他各部門。
     qualified and experienced staffs, training courses and related budget for
     risk management and internal audit.


     The Board of Directors, as the risk management supreme leading organ         董事會是本公司風險管理的最高領導機構，對整體風
     of the Company, undertakes ultimate responsibility for establishing,         險管理體系的建設和有效運行負最終責任，其負責整
     improvement and effective operation of the overall risk management           體風險管理體系的建立、健全和有效運轉，並負責明
     system, takes charge of the building, improvement and efficient operation    確高級管理層、各部門在風險管理體系中的職責分
     of the overall risk management system and has the discretion to delegate     工。
     senior management and operational division of each department in the
     risk management system.


     The Board of Directors is responsible for evaluating and determining the     董事會負責評估及釐定本公司達成戰略目標時所願意
     nature and extent of the risks the Company is willing to take in achieving   接納的風險性質及程度，並確保本公司設立及維持合
     its strategic objectives, and ensuring that the Company establishes          適且有效的風險管理及內部監控系統。董事會下設審
     and maintains appropriate and effective risk management and internal         核委員會，負責監察本公司風險管理系統及內部監督

     is responsible for monitoring the confirmation of the appropriateness        事會提供有關系統是否有效的確認。

IMPLEMENTATION OF RISK MANAGEMENT AND                                         風險管理和內部監控工作開展
INTERNAL CONTROL
The Board has reviewed the comprehensive risk management policies             董事會已審閱本公司所採取的綜合風險管理政策，該
adopted by the Company, which include a risk management framework.            政策包括一項風險管理框架，根據內部環境、風險評
Based on the internal control elements of internal environment, risk          估、控制活動、資訊與溝通及內部監督等內部監控元
assessment, control activities, information and communication, and            素，對財務監控、運作監控及合規監控等所有重要監
internal supervision, the framework assesses all material aspects of          控方面進行評估，以持續識別、評估、分析和監控與
supervision including financial, operational and compliance supervision,      我們戰略目標相關的主要風險。本公司的高級管理層
so as to continuously identify, assess, analyse and monitor the key           乃至董事均監督風險管理政策的實施。已識別的風險
risks associated with the Company’s strategic objectives. The senior          會根據其可能性和影響範圍進行分析，由管理層妥為
management and even the Directors of the Company supervise the                跟進並作出糾正，並向董事會報告。
implementation of the risk management policies. Identified risks are
analysed by reference to their likelihood of occurrence and scope of
impact, properly followed up and rectified by the management, and
reported to the Board.


The Company’s Internal Control Department and Internal Audit                  本公司內部控制部門與內部審計部門均定期審查和討
Department regularly review and discuss the Company’s annual risk             論公司的年度風險評估，並監督與我們業務相關的風
assessment, and supervise the implementation of risk control measures         險控制措施落地情況。經過風險評估，2025年度風險
related to the Company’s business. Following the risk assessment, the         主要涉及食品安全風險和市場風險，我們根據企業實
major risks for the year 2025 are food safety risks and market risks. The     際情況制定了切實可行的應對方案對風險進行有效管
Company has formulated practical and feasible response plans based on         理：
its actual situation to effectively manage such risks.


Food Safety Risks: Food safety and product quality are the cornerstones       食品安全風險：食品安全與產品質量是本集團聲譽與
of the Group’s reputation and sustainable development. Such risk runs         永續發展的基石。此風險貫穿於從研發、採購、生產
through the entire value chain from R&D, procurement and production           到儲運、銷售的全鏈條。任何環節的疏忽，例如未能
to storage, transportation and sales. Negligence in any link, such as         嚴格遵循最新的法律法規與監管要求對原料及成品進
failure to strictly comply with the latest laws, regulations and regulatory   行檢驗，或未能對採購、生產及質檢流程實施標準化
requirements to inspect raw materials and finished products, or failure       與有效管控，均可能引發食品安全負面事件，從而導
to standardise and effectively control the procurement, production            致品牌聲譽受損、客戶流失及直接的經濟損失。
and quality inspection processes, may give rise to negative food safety
incidents, which in turn may result in damage to brand reputation,

of loyal fans. Meanwhile, under the product development philosophy of         化思維，讓美食原點的價值最大化釋放」的產品開發

Printing will provide food packaging materials to our Group.

can be renewed upon its expiry as agreed by the parties to the agreement.     滿時續簽。

To the shareholders of WEILONG Delicious Global Holdings Ltd                     致衛龍美味全球控股有限公司股東

OPINION                                                                         意見
We have audited the consolidated financial statements of WEILONG                我們已審計衛龙美味全球控股有限公司（以下簡稱「貴
Delicious Global Holdings Ltd (the “Company”) and its subsidiaries (the         公司」）及其附屬公司（以下統稱「貴集團」）列載於第
“Group”) set out on pages 108 to 212, which comprise the consolidated           108至212頁的合併財務報表，包括於2025年12月31
statement of financial position as at 31 December 2025, and the                 日的合併財務狀況表、合併損益表、合併綜合收益
consolidated statement of profit or loss, the consolidated statement of         表、合併權益變動表及截至該日止年度的合併現金流
comprehensive income, the consolidated statement of changes in equity           量表，以及合併財務報表附註，包括主要會計政策信
and the consolidated statement of cash flows for the year then ended,           息。
and notes to the consolidated financial statements, including material
accounting policy information.


In our opinion, the consolidated financial statements give a true and           我們認為，該等合併財務報表已根據國際會計準則理
fair view of the consolidated financial position of the Group as at 31          事會（「國際會計準則理事會」）頒佈的《國際財務報告
December 2025, and of its consolidated financial performance and its            準則》會計準則真實而中肯地反映了     貴集團於2025
consolidated cash flows for the year then ended in accordance with IFRS         年12月31日的合併財務狀況及其截至該日止年度的合
Accounting Standards as issued by the International Accounting Standards        併財務表現及合併現金流量，並已遵照香港《公司條
Board (“IASB”) and have been properly prepared in compliance with the           例》的披露規定妥為擬備。
disclosure requirements of the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.


BASIS FOR OPINION                                                               意見的基礎

THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS
Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether the          我們的目標，是對合併財務報表整體是否不存在由於
consolidated financial statements as a whole are free from material          欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述取得合理保證，並
misstatement, whether due to fraud or error, and to issue an auditor’s       出具包括我們意見的核數師報告。我們僅向          閣下
report that includes our opinion. Our report is made solely to you, as a     （作為整體）作出報告，除此之外本報告別無其他目
body, and for no other purpose. We do not assume responsibility towards      的。我們不會就本報告的內容向任何其他人士負上或
or accept liability to any other person for the contents of this report.     承擔任何責任。

Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance, but is not a guarantee    合理保證是高水平的保證，但不能保證按照《國際審
that an audit conducted in accordance with ISAs will always detect a         計準則》進行的審計，在某一重大錯誤陳述存在時總
material misstatement when it exists. Misstatements can arise from fraud     能發現。錯誤陳述可以由欺詐或錯誤引起，如果合理
or error and are considered material if, individually or in the aggregate,   預期它們單獨或匯總起來可能影響合併財務報表使用
they could reasonably be expected to influence the economic decisions of     者依賴合併財務報表所作出的經濟決定，則有關的錯
users taken on the basis of these consolidated financial statements.         誤陳述可被視作重大。

As part of an audit in accordance with ISAs, we exercise professional        在根據《國際審計準則》進行審計的過程中，我們運用
judgement and maintain professional scepticism throughout the audit.         了專業判斷，保持了專業懷疑態度。我們亦：
We also:


•    Identify and assess the risks of material misstatement of the           •   識別和評估由於欺詐或錯誤而導致合併財務報表
     consolidated financial statements, whether due to fraud or error,           存在重大錯誤陳述的風險，設計及執行審計程序
     design and perform audit procedures responsive to those risks,              以應對這些風險，以及獲取充足和適當的審計憑
     and obtain audit evidence that is sufficient and appropriate to             證，作為我們意見的基礎。由於欺詐可能涉及串
     provide a basis for our opinion. The risk of not detecting a material       謀、偽造、蓄意遺漏、虛假陳述，或凌駕於內部
     misstatement resulting from fraud is higher than for one resulting          控制之上，因此未能發現因欺詐而導致的重大錯
     from error, as fraud may involve collusion, forgery, intentional            誤陳述的風險高於未能發現因錯誤而導致的重大
     omissions, misrepresentations, or the override of internal control.         錯誤陳述的風險。

      1. GENERAL INFORMATION                                                      1. 一般資料
           WEILONG Delicious Global Holdings Ltd (the “Company”) was                衛龙美味全球控股有限公司（「本公司」）於2018
           incorporated in the Cayman Islands on 6 July 2018 as an exempted         年7月6日根據開曼群島法律第22章《公司法》
           company with limited liability under the Company Act (Cap 22, Law       （1961年第3號法例，經合併及修訂）於開曼群島
           3 of 1961 as consolidated and revised) of the Cayman Islands. The        註冊成立為豁免有限公司。其註冊辦事處位於
           address of its registered office is the offices of Maples Corporate      Maples Corporate Services Limited的辦事處（地
           Services Limited, PO Box 309, Ugland House, Grand Cayman, KY1-           址為PO Box 309, Ugland House, Grand Cayman,
           1104, Cayman Islands.                                                    KY1-1104, Cayman Islands）。

           The Company is an investment holding company. The Company and            本公司為一家投資控股公司。本公司及其附屬公
           its subsidiaries (together, the “Group”) are principally engaged in      司（統稱「本集團」）主要在中華人民共和國（「中
           the production and sale of spicy snack food in the People’s Republic     國」）從事辣味休閒食品的生產和銷售。
           of China (“PRC”).


           The ultimate holding company of the Company is HH Global Capital         本公司的最終控股公司為和和全球資本有限公
           Ltd. The ultimate controlling parties are Mr. Liu Weiping and his        司。最終控制方為劉衛平先生及其弟弟劉福平先
           brother Mr. Liu Fuping (the “Controlling Shareholders”), who             生（「控股股東」），彼等亦分別為本公司董事會主
           are also the chairman and the executive director of the board of         席及執行董事。
           directors of the Company, respectively.

           “Listing”) by way of its initial public offering (“IPO”).                公司主板上市（「上市」）。

2. SUMMARY OF MATERIAL ACCOUNTING                                             2. 重大會計政策概要（續）
   POLICIES (Cont’d)
    2.1 Basis of preparation                                                    2.1 編製基準
        The consolidated financial statements of the Group have                   本集團合併財務報表乃按照國際會計準則
        been prepared in accordance with IFRS Accounting Standards                理事會（「國際會計準則理事會」）頒佈的《國
        as issued by the International Accounting Standards Board                 際財務報告準則》會計準則及《香港公司條
        (“IASB”) and the disclosure requirements of the Hong Kong                 例》的披露規定編製。合併財務報表根據歷
        Companies Ordinance. The consolidated financial statements                史成本慣例進行編製，惟若干金融資產乃
        have been prepared under the historical cost convention,                  按公允價值計量。除另有說明外，該等合
        except for certain financial assets which have been measured              併財務報表以人民幣（「人民幣」）呈列，且
        at fair value. These consolidated financial statements are                所有價值均約整至最接近千位。
        presented in Renminbi (“RMB”) and all values are rounded to
        nearest thousand, unless otherwise stated.


        The preparation of consolidated financial statements in                   根據《國際財務報告準則》會計準則編製合
        conformity with IFRS Accounting Standards requires the                    併財務報表需使用若干關鍵會計估計，同
        use of certain critical accounting estimates. It also requires            時要求管理層在應用本集團會計政策過程
        management to exercise its judgement in the process of                    中作出判斷。涉及更高程度判斷或複雜性
        applying the Group’s accounting policies. The areas involving             的方面或假設及估計對合併財務報表屬重
        a higher degree of judgement or complexity, or areas where                大的方面於附註4內披露。
        assumptions and estimates are significant to the consolidated
        financial statements are disclosed in Note 4.


    2.2 Changes in accounting policies and disclosures                          2.2 會計政策及披露事項變動

    2.4 Principles of consolidation and equity accounting                       2.4 合併原則及權益會計處理
        (a)   Subsidiaries                                                        (a)   附屬公司
              Subsidiaries are all entities (including structured entities)             附屬公司乃本集團擁有控制權的所有
              over which the Group has control. The Group controls                      實體（包括結構實體）。當本集團因參
              an entity when the Group is exposed to, or has rights                     與某實體而承受該實體可變回報或享
              to, variable returns from its involvement with the entity                 有該等可變回報的權利，並有能力通
              and has the ability to affect those returns through its                   過其指導該實體活動的權力影響該等
              power to direct the activities of the entity. Subsidiaries                回報時，本集團即擁有對該實體的控
              are fully consolidated from the date on which control is                  制權。附屬公司自控制權轉移至本集
              transferred to the Group. They are deconsolidated from                    團之日起全面合併。自控制權終止之
              the date that control ceases.                                             日起停止合併。

              The acquisition method of accounting is used to account                   會計收購法用於列賬不在本集團共同
              for business combinations not under common control by                     控制下的業務合併。
              the Group.


              Intercompany transactions, balances and unrealized                        已將公司間交易、集團各公司間交易
              gains on transactions between group companies are                         結餘及未變現收益剔除。除非交易提
              eliminated. Unrealized losses are also eliminated unless                  供了所轉讓資產減值的證據，否則還
              the transaction provides evidence of an impairment of the                 應剔除未變現虧損。附屬公司的會計
              transferred asset. Accounting policies of subsidiaries have               政策已在需要時作出更改，以確保與
              been changed where necessary to ensure consistency                        本集團所採納政策一致。
              with the policies adopted by the Group.


              Non-controlling interests in the results and equity of                    附屬公司業績及權益中的非控股權益
              subsidiaries are shown separately in the consolidated                     單獨呈列於合併財務報表中。
              financial statements.

    2.5 Foreign currency translation                                         2.5 外幣換算
        (a)   Functional and presentation currency                             (a)   功能及呈列貨幣
              Items included in the consolidated financial statements                本集團各實體的合併財務報表所列項
              of each of the Group’s entities are measured using                     目均以該實體營運所在地主要經濟環
              the currency of the primary economic environment in                    境的貨幣（「功能貨幣」）計量。本公司
              which the entity operates (“the functional currency”).                 的功能貨幣為港元（「港元」），而由於
              The Company’s functional currency is Hong Kong Dollar                  本集團主要於中國內地經營，本集團
              (“HKD”), while the Group’s presentation currency is                    的呈列貨幣為人民幣（「人民幣」）。
              Renminbi (“RMB”) as the Group mainly operates in the
              Chinese Mainland.


        (b)   Transactions and balances                                        (b)   交易及結餘
              Foreign currency transactions are translated into the                  外幣交易按交易當日的匯率換算為功
              functional currency using the exchange rates at the dates              能貨幣。該等交易結算產生的外匯收

                 (d)   Impairment                                                            (d)   減值
                       The Group assesses on a forward-looking basis the                           本集團按前瞻性基準評估與按攤銷成
                       expected credit loss associated with its debt instruments                   本及以公允價值計量且其變動計入其
                       carried at amortized cost and FVOCI. The impairment                         他綜合收益列賬的債務工具有關的預
                       methodology applied depends on whether there has been                       期信貸虧損。所應用的減值方法取決
                       a significant increase in credit risk.                                      於信貸風險是否大幅增加。

                       For trade receivables, impairment is measured as lifetime                   貿易應收款項的減值按存續期預期虧
                       expected loss.                                                              損計量。

                       For other receivables, impairment is measured as either                     其他應收款項按12個月預期信貸虧損
                       12-month expected credit loss or lifetime expected                          或存續期預期信貸虧損計量減值，視
                       credit loss, depending on whether there has been a                          乎自初始確認以來信貸風險是否大幅
                       significant increase in credit risk since initial recognition.              增加而定。倘應收款項的信貸風險自
                       If a significant increase in credit risk of a receivable has                初步確認後大幅增加，則減值按存續
                       occurred since initial recognition, then impairment is                      期預期信貸虧損計量。
                       measured as lifetime expected credit losses.


           2.10 Inventories                                                               2.10 存貨
                 Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net realizable              存貨按成本與可變現淨值兩者中的較低者
                 value. Cost comprises direct materials, direct labour and                   入賬。成本包括直接材料支出、直接人工
                 an appropriate proportion of variable and fixed overhead                    支出及適當比例的可變及固定間接費用，
                 expenditure, the latter being allocated based on normal                     後者基於正常營運能力分配。成本基於加
                 operating capacity. Costs are assigned to individual items of               權平均成本分配予個別存貨項目。購買存
                 inventory on the basis of weighted average costs. Costs of                  貨的成本經扣除回扣及折扣後釐定。可變
                 purchased inventory are determined after deducting rebates                  現淨值為日常業務過程中的估計售價減估
                 and discounts. Net realizable value is the estimated selling price          計完成成本及進行銷售所需的估計成本。
                 in the ordinary course of business less the estimated costs of
                 completion and the estimated costs necessary to make the sale.

    2.18 Leases                                                                 2.18 租賃
        Leases are recognized as a right-of-use asset and a                        租賃於租賃資產可供本集團使用之日確認
        corresponding liability at the date at which the leased asset is           為使用權資產及有關負債。
        available for use by the Group.


        Contracts may contain both lease and non-lease components.                 合同可能包括租賃和非租賃部分。本集團
        The Group allocates the consideration in the contract to the               將合同中的對價按相對獨立價格基準分配
        lease and non-lease components based on their relative stand-              至租賃及非租賃部分。然而，就本集團作
        alone prices. However, for leases of real estate for which the             為承租人的房地產租賃而言，本集團已選
        Group is a lessee, it has elected not to separate lease and non-           擇不分開租賃和非租賃部分，而是將彼等
        lease components and instead accounts for these as a single                作為單一租賃部分入賬。
        lease component.


        Assets and liabilities arising from a lease are initially measured         租賃產生的資產及負債初步按現值基準計
        on a present value basis. Lease liabilities include the net present        量。租賃負債包括以下租賃付款之淨現值：
        value of the following lease payments:


        •     fixed payments (including in-substance fixed payments),              •   固定付款（包括實質固定付款）減任何
              less any lease incentives receivable                                     應收租賃獎勵

        •     variable lease payment that are based on an index or a               •   基於指數或利率的可變租賃付款，最
              rate, initially measured using the index or rate as at the               初以租賃起始日的指數或利率計量
              commencement date


        •     amounts expected to be payable by the Group under                    •   預期本集團於剩餘價值擔保下應付的
              residual value guarantees                                                款項

        •     the exercise price of a purchase option if the Group is              •   購買選擇權的行使價（倘本集團合理
              reasonably certain to exercise that option, and                          確定將行使該項選擇權），及

        •     payments of penalties for terminating the lease, if the              •   支付終止租賃的罰款（倘租期反映本
              lease term reflects the Group exercising that option.                    集團行使該項選擇權）。

        Lease payments to be made under reasonably certain extension               根據合理確定延期選擇權作出的租賃付款
        options are also included in the measurement of the liability.             也包括在負債的計量中。

                 The lease payments are discounted using the interest rate                租賃付款採用租賃有關的隱含利率貼現。

                 use asset in a similar economic environment with similar terms,

                      by the individual lessee as a starting point, adjusted to                第三方融資為出發點進行調整，以反
                      reflect changes in financing conditions since third party                映自獲得第三方融資以來融資條件的
                      financing was received                                                   變動

                      interest rate adjusted for credit risk for leases held by                租賃的信貸風險（最近並無第三方融

                      financing, and

                      country, currency and security.                                          家、貨幣及抵押。

                 lease payments based on an index or rate, which are not                  租賃付款，而有關指數或利率在生效前不

      2. SUMMARY OF MATERIAL ACCOUNTING                                                  2. 重大會計政策概要（續）
         POLICIES (Cont’d)
           2.23 Fair value measurement                                                     2.23 公允價值計量
                 The Group measures certain of its financial assets and financial            本集團於報告期末按公允價值計量其若干
                 liabilities at fair value at the end of the reporting period. Fair          金融資產及金融負債。公允價值為市場參
                 value is the price that would be received to sell an asset or               與者於計量日期在有序交易中出售資產所
                 paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between              收取的價格或轉讓負債所支付的價格。公
                 market participants at the measurement date. The fair value                 允價值計量乃根據假設出售資產或轉讓負
                 measurement is based on the presumption that the transaction                債的交易於資產或負債主要市場或（在無主
                 to sell the asset or transfer the liability takes place either in the       要市場情況下）最具優勢市場進行而作出。
                 principal market for the asset or liability, or in the absence of a         主要或最具優勢市場須為本集團可進入的
                 principal market, in the most advantageous market for the asset             市場。資產或負債的公允價值乃按假設市
                 or liability. The principal or the most advantageous market                 場參與者於資產或負債定價時會以最佳經
                 must be accessible by the Group. The fair value of an asset                 濟利益行事計量。
                 or a liability is measured using the assumptions that market
                 participants would use when pricing the asset or liability,
                 assuming that market participants act in their economic best
                 interest.


                 The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate in                 本集團採用適合具體情況並且有足夠可用
                 the circumstances and for which sufficient data are available to            數據支持的估值技術，並盡可能多地使用
                 measure fair value, maximizing the use of relevant observable               相關可觀察輸入值，盡可能少地使用不可
                 inputs and minimizing the use of unobservable inputs, see Note              觀察輸入值（見下文附註3.3）。
                 3.3 below.

    3.3 Fair value estimation                                                         3.3 公允價值估計
        (a)   Fair value hierarchy                                                        (a)   公允價值層級
              To provide an indication about the reliability of the inputs                      為得出有關釐定公允價值所用輸入數
              used in determining fair value, the Group has classified                          據之可靠性指標，本集團已按會計準
              its financial instruments into the three levels prescribed                        則規定將其金融工具分為三個層級。
              under the accounting standards. An explanation of each                            下表為對各層級的說明。
              follows the table below.


              Level 1: The fair value of financial instruments traded in                        第一層級：在活躍市場買賣的金融工
              active markets (such as publicly traded derivatives, and                          具（例如公開買賣的衍生工具及股本
              equity securities) is based on quoted market prices at the                        證券）的公允價值根據報告期末的市
              end of the reporting period. The quoted market price used                         場報價列賬。本集團持有的金融資產
              for financial assets held by the Group is the current bid                         所使用的市場報價為現時買盤價。該
              price. These instruments are included in level 1.                                 等工具計入第一層級。

              Level 2: The fair value of financial instruments that                             第二層級：並非於活躍市場買賣的金
              are not traded in an active market (for example, over-                            融工具（例如場外衍生工具）的公允價
              the-counter derivatives) is determined using valuation                            值乃使用估值技術釐定，該等估值技
              techniques which maximize the use of observable market                            術盡量利用可觀察市場數據，並盡可
              data and rely as little as possible on entity-specific                            能減少依賴實體特定的估計。倘計算
              estimates. If all significant inputs required to fair value an                    工具公允價值所需的所有重大輸入數
              instrument are observable, the instrument is included in                          據均屬可觀察，則將工具計入第二層
              level 2.                                                                          級。

              Level 3: If one or more of the significant inputs are                             第三層級：倘一項或多項重大輸入數
              not based on observable market data, the instrument                               據並非按可觀察市場數據得出，則該

              securities.                                                                       適用該等情況。

                       period.

4. SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING ESTIMATES AND                                      4. 重大會計估計及判斷
   JUDGEMENTS
    Estimates and judgements are continually evaluated. They are based         持續評估估計及判斷。估計及判斷乃基於歷史經
    on historical experience and other factors, including expectations of      驗及其他因素，包括可能對實體產生財務影響並
    future events that may have a financial impact on the entity and that      於此等情況下被視為合理之未來事件之預期。
    are believed to be reasonable under the circumstances.


    Deferred income tax assets                                                 遞延所得稅資產
    Deferred income tax assets are recognized for unused tax losses and        遞延所得稅資產於有可能出現可利用該等虧損及
    deductible temporary differences to the extent that it is probable         可抵扣暫時差額予以抵扣的應課稅利潤時，就未
    that taxable profit will be available against which the losses and         動用稅項虧損及可抵扣暫時差額確認。釐定可予
    deductible temporary differences can be utilized. Significant              確認的遞延所得稅資產金額時，管理層須根據日
    management judgement is required to determine the amount of                後應課稅利潤可能產生的時間及水平，配合未來
    deferred income tax assets that can be recognized, based upon the          稅項規劃策略作出重大判斷。於2025年12月31
    likely timing and level of future taxable profits together with future     日，與已確認稅項虧損及可抵扣暫時差額相關的
    tax planning strategies. The carrying value of deferred income tax         遞延所得稅資產賬面值為人民幣120,388,000元
    assets relating to recognized tax losses and deductible temporary         （2024年：人民幣99,610,000元）。進一步詳情
    differences at 31 December 2025 was RMB120,388,000 (2024:                  載於財務報表附註31。
    RMB99,610,000). Further details are contained in Note 31 to the
    financial statements.


    Variable consideration for volume rebates                                  批量返利的可變代價
    The Group estimates variable consideration to be included in the           本集團就將計入銷售具批量返利的產品交易價格
    transaction price for the sales of products with volume rebates.           的可變代價進行估計。

    The Group’s volume rebates are analysed on a per customer basis for        本集團的批量返利乃就設有單一批量限額的合同
    contracts that are subject to a single volume threshold. Determining       按每名客戶進行分析。釐定客戶是否可能獲得返
    whether a customer is likely to be entitled to a rebate depends            利取決於客戶過往獲得返利的情況及迄今累計的
    on the customer’s historical rebate entitlement and accumulated            採購額。
    purchases to date.


    The Group updates its assessment of expected volume rebates yearly         本集團每年更新其預期批量返利評估，及就退款
    and the refund liabilities are adjusted accordingly. Estimates of          負債作相應調整。預期年度批量返利的估計對情
    expected volume rebates are sensitive to changes in circumstances          況變化相當敏感，而本集團過往有關返利支付情
    and the Group’s past experience regarding rebate entitlements may          況的經驗可能無法代表客戶未來獲得實際返利的
    not be representative of a customer’s actual rebate entitlements in        情況。
    the future.

           The Group is principally engaged in the production and sale of spicy     本集團主要從事辣味休閒食品的生產及銷售。本
           snack food. Majority of the Group’s revenue and business activities      集團的大部分收入及業務活動均於中國進行。
           are conducted in the PRC.

           units based on their products and has three reportable operating         及擁有以下三個可呈報經營分部：

                 Fengchi Kelp


           •     Other products, primarily comprising Spicy Tofu Skin, gift box     •   其他產品，主要包括香辣豆皮、禮盒產品
                 products and others                                                    等

           The chief operating decision-maker (“CODM”) monitors the results         主要營運決策者（「主要營運決策者」）監察本集團
           of the Group’s operating segments separately for the purpose             各經營分部的業績，以作出有關資源分配及績效

           assessment. Segment performance is evaluated based on gross              要營運決策者並未為資源分配和績效評估而定期
           profit. No analysis of segment operating profit is presented as          審查此類信息，因此未呈列對分部經營利潤的分
           CODM does not regularly review such information for the purposes         析。分部資產及負債未呈列，因為主要營運決策
           of resource allocation and performance assessment. Segment assets        者會集中審查資產及負債。因此，僅呈列分部收

10. SHARE-BASED PAYMENTS                                                     10. 以股份為基礎的付款

           The recoverable amount of the konjac raw materials processing           魔芋原材料加工現金產生單位的可收回金額按使
           cash-generating unit has been determined based on a value in use        用價值計算法確定，該計算法使用以高級管理層
           calculation using cash flow projections based on financial budgets      批准為期五年的財務預算為基準的現金流量預
           covering a five-year period approved by senior management. The          測。適用於現金流量預測的貼現率為16%，五
           discount rate applied to the cash flow projections was 16% and cash     年以上的現金流量採用增長率4%推斷，該增長
           flows beyond the five-year period were extrapolated using a growth      率與相關行業的長期平均增長率相同。
           rate of 4%, which was the same as the long-term average growth
           rate of the relevant industry.
